  - `default:` in the `@/hooks/useSeasonalTheme` mock of 11 files (6 `MassScoreEntryTool` tests, `admin-score-tools`, `MatchesTable`, `SeasonForm`, `TeamManagementTab`, `TeamMembershipApprovalTab`). Proof: the module has no default export (two named exports) and nothing imports it as a default.

It is removed because no production file imports it from the barrel and none of the 6 hooks under test reaches it; `mocks.bracket` was used on that one line only.

They now use `expect(() => ...).toThrow(...)` with the same checks. Mutations: `handleDatabaseError` returns instead of throwing (the 2 old try/catch tests pass, the new ones fail); `ensureFound` returns instead of throwing (the 3 old ones pass, the new ones fail); the context, the hint or the identifier is dropped (the new tests fail).

Title fixed and marked "today's behaviour"; `expect(bye.id).toBe('a')` added next to the old `toBeDefined()`. Mutation: `<` becomes `<=`; the old test passes, the new one fails ('c' instead of 'a'). This pins the code. **It is not a league rule**: nobody decided that the first of equal teams sits out.
- **B4: two new tests for the career rankings.**
  - `computeAllTeamsTotals.test.ts`, "works out career power score from the old seasons and the current-season row". Two teams, a title and a runner-up in a 0.7 division, a stale stored row for the current season that must be left out, and a current-season row from the Teams list. `career_power_score` is worked out by hand in a comment: `(680 + 590 + 720) / 30 + 5.39 = 71.7233` and `(680 + 590 + 480) / 26 + 5.39 = 72.6977`. The first run matched the hand figures. Six mutations each fail it: the power score not passed on, the floored score not passed on, wins dropped, the stale current-season row counted again (the test then reads 60.14, which is also the hand figure for that case), the bonus not squared, the current score scaled x100.

## Slice 3D + 3G result (built 2026-11-18 on `claude/adoring-bohr-waxxok`; 2 code commits + 1 docs commit; no migration, no SQL, nothing to hand-apply)

**Answer first.** Six components no longer import a service. Their 8 data reads moved into 5 small hooks. **Nothing changes on screen.** Every query key, query function and `enabled` rule is the same text as before, so the cache and every refresh trigger behave as they did. Three new test files (12 tests) pin the part that could break quietly: which list is fetched, and which key a refresh hits.

- **Commits.** 3D `85171b6` (hero-card reads). 3G `20a1cc0` (playoff reads). This docs commit. Each code commit is one revert on its own. After QA, one more tests-only commit (`045e354`) pins when final standings are asked for (see the last test bullet below).
- **What moved (8 reads, 6 components, 5 new hooks).** Each hook returns the raw `useQuery` result and imports only `useQuery` from react-query as a value.
  - `src/hooks/hero-cards/useTargetEntityOptions.ts` (param `targetType`; three `useQuery` calls in the old order: teams, divisions, seasons). Used by `TargetEntitySelector` in `TargetSelector.tsx`.
  - `src/hooks/hero-cards/useTeamsForChampions.ts` (`hiddenDivisionIds`, `enabled`). Used by `ChampionsEditor.tsx`.
  - `src/hooks/hero-cards/useChampionTeams.ts` (takes `championsMap`, works out the ids inside). Used by `ChampionsHeroCard.tsx`.
  - `src/hooks/brackets/useFinalStandings.ts` (`bracketId`, `enabled`). Used by `FinalStandings.tsx` and `BracketAdminToolbar.tsx`. **Each caller passes its own `enabled` expression, unchanged:** `show && Boolean(bracketId)` and `Boolean(bracketId) && isCompleted`. The toolbar still reads `data` only and is not gated on `isLoading`.
  - `src/hooks/brackets/useBracketParticipants.ts` (`bracketId`). Used by `BracketDetail.tsx`.
- **Keys unchanged (the 7 key strings).** `['teams-for-selector']`, `['divisions-for-selector']`, `['seasons-for-selector']`, `['teams-for-champions', hiddenDivisionIds]`, `['champions', championIds]`, `['final-standings', bracketId]`, `['bracket-participants', bracketId]`. A grep of `src` and `tests` before and after shows the same `queryKey` literals; the only differences are that each moved from a component into a hook, the two `final-standings` uses became one, and the new tests add their own `invalidateQueries` lines. The two places that refresh these keys were not touched: `src/hooks/useRecalculateStandings.ts:26` (`['final-standings', bracketId]`) and `src/components/playoffs/SeedingUpdateDialog.tsx:233` (`['bracket-participants', bracketId]`).
- **Tests added (3 new files in the first two commits; no existing test file edited then).**
  - `src/components/admin/hero-cards/__tests__/TargetSelector.test.tsx` (4 tests, real component, picker never opened): a team card asks only for teams, a division card only for divisions, a season card only for seasons, and "none" shows nothing and asks for nothing.

## Slice 3E + 3F result (2026-11-18)

- **What moved.** Four screens stopped importing a service. Each call now goes through a tiny hook that returns the service function itself.
  - `ForgotPassword` -> `src/hooks/auth/usePasswordReset.ts` (`{ sendResetEmail }`).
  - `ResetPassword` -> `src/hooks/auth/useUpdatePassword.ts` (`{ setNewPassword }`). The hook is called above the early returns.
  - `ProfileForm` -> `src/hooks/profile/useProfileActions.ts` (`{ checkUsername, saveProfile }`). New folder `src/hooks/profile/`.
  - `TeamEditSection` -> `src/hooks/teams/useUpdateTeamDetails.ts` (returns `updateTeamNameAndImage`). Called above the early `return null`. Not added to the `src/hooks/teams/index.ts` barrel (it exports only `useTeamsQuery`; the new hook is imported by file path).
- **Plain pass-through on purpose: no `useMutation`, no `useQuery`.**
  - `useMutation` pauses while the phone is offline. A password change would then fire late, when the network came back.
  - `useMutation` keeps its variables in the mutation cache. That would keep the new password in memory.
  - `ForgotPassword`, `ResetPassword` and `ProfileForm` tests render with no `QueryClientProvider`. A mutation hook would crash them.
  - The hooks import only the service: no react-query, no logger, no `useToast`.
- **What stayed in the components.** All state, validation, `try/catch/finally`, toasts, `navigate`, error text and double-submit guards. In `ProfileForm`: the 500 ms debounce, `latestCheckIdRef`, the `useEffectEvent` handler, `recheckCount`, the derived flags, `form.setError` / `clearErrors`, the awaited save and the un-awaited `onProfileUpdated()`. In `TeamEditSection`: its own `useQueryClient` and the same order (service, toast, `setIsEditing(false)`, `await refreshMembership()`, invalidate `['teams']`, invalidate `['team-details', id]`).
- **Keys unchanged.** The two invalidate keys in `TeamEditSection` are byte for byte the same. No other cache key is touched. No service file changed.
- **Tests.** None added, none edited. `ForgotPassword.test.tsx`, `ResetPassword.test.tsx`, `ProfileForm.test.tsx` and `TeamEditSection.test.tsx` already mock the same service paths and pin the wiring; they pass as they were. `TESTING.md` counts do not change.
- **Not changed on purpose.**
  - **DONE in PR-3 (see "Slice 3 PR-3 result" below):** `updateTeamNameAndImage` had no row check (a blocked row could show "Team Updated" with nothing saved). It now throws when no row was updated. A behavior change on its own.
  - 3H-3J (`SeedingUpdateDialog`, `BracketCreationDialog`, `MassScoreEntryTool`) were still pending then and needed Doug's OK first. `BracketCreationDialog` (3I) and `SeedingUpdateDialog` (3H) are done since: see "Slice 3I result" and "Slice 3H result". **3J is DONE too, see "Slice 3J result".**
- **Recount.** 44 non-test files in `src/components` and `src/pages` still import `@/services` (48 before this slice). 23 of the 44 import only types; 21 import a real value (INFERRED mix, not sorted: the 3H-3J files, bracket-admin helpers, colocated hooks and helpers). The lint rule "components must not import services" still cannot go on. (Done since: 3H and 3J; see "Slice 3H result" and "Slice 3J result" for the later counts.)
- **Rollback.** Revert either commit. Nothing is stored, nothing to hand-apply.

## Slice 3J result (built 2026-11-20 on `claude/adoring-bohr-waxxok`; 1 code commit + 1 docs commit; no migration, no SQL, nothing to hand-apply)

**Answer first.** The Mass Score Entry tool no longer calls the delete service itself. The call moved into a new hook, `useDeleteMatchWithReversal`. **Nothing changes for admins.** The same service function runs with the same match id, and the same database function reverses the stats in one transaction. The service and the database function were not touched. The only change is who calls the service. This is the last slice of the slice-3 series.

- **What moved.**
  - New `src/hooks/matches/updates/useDeleteMatchWithReversal.ts`: a `useMutation` whose `mutationFn` is `(matchId) => deleteMatchWithStatsReversal(matchId)`. No `onSuccess`, no `onError`, no toast, no refresh, no `errorLog`, no `retry` option. It is not added to `src/hooks/matches/updates/index.ts` (nothing needs it there).
  - `src/components/admin/MassScoreEntryTool.tsx`: `const deleteMatch = useDeleteMatchWithReversal();` and `await deleteMatch.mutateAsync(deleteMatchId)`. The `@/services` import is gone, so the tool has zero `@/services` imports. Everything else is the same: its own `isDeleting` state (not switched to `isPending`), the comment, `removeMatch(id)`, then the success toast, then `invalidateAllDataQueries(queryClient)`, then `setDeleteMatchId(null)`, the catch (`errorLog` plus the red toast with `getUIErrorMessage`) and the `finally`.
  - `useMatchDelete.ts` is a separate hook for another screen. It was not changed and not reused.
- **Design choice 1: an arrow wrapper, not a bare reference.** TanStack calls `mutationFn(variables, context)`. A bare `mutationFn: deleteMatchWithStatsReversal` would pass a second argument to the service. The service takes one argument, so it would still work today, but a test now pins "one argument only" so a later change to the service cannot be hit by surprise.
- **Design choice 2: `networkMode: 'always'`.** TanStack v5 pauses a mutation while the browser is offline. Before this slice the tool called the service directly, so an offline delete failed at once and showed the red toast. Without this line the dialog would hang on "deleting", and the delete could run later, when the admin is back online and no longer expects it. Same fix as 3H.
- **Design choice 3: the refresh and the order stay in the tool.** The hook does not refresh anything. The row is removed, then the toast shows, then the data refreshes, then the dialog closes. The dialog closes on success only, so a failed delete leaves the prompt to retry from. A new test pins the order.
- **One new side effect (harmless).** The delete now runs through `useMutation`, so the app-wide `MutationCache` `onError` in `src/App.tsx` also sees a failed delete. It adds one `mutation_error` metric and one `errorLog("Mutation failed")` line. It shows no extra toast. The tool still logs `Failed to delete match:` itself, so **a failed delete now writes two log lines** (before: one).
- **Mutation checks (each run on the real file, then restored byte for byte; `cmp` and `git diff` confirmed).**
  - (i) `mutationFn` changed to the bare reference: the hook test "calls the service once with the match id and no extra argument" fails. The existing "routes delete through the atomic RPC" component test fails too (`toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith('m1')` sees the extra argument). The other tests stay green.
  - (ii) the `networkMode: 'always'` line removed: both offline tests fail (the paused delete never reaches the service). The other 10 tests stay green.
  - (iii) an `invalidateQueries` added to the hook: "does not refresh any query itself" fails. The other 11 stay green.
  - (iv) in the tool, the toast moved below `invalidateAllDataQueries`: the new order test fails. The other 3 stay green.
  - (v) in the tool, `setDeleteMatchId(null)` moved into the `finally`: the existing "on RPC failure ... " test fails (it checks that the dialog stays open). The other 3 stay green.
- **Tests added: 9.** `src/hooks/matches/updates/__tests__/useDeleteMatchWithReversal.test.ts` (8 tests, service mocked): the service is called once with the id and with exactly one argument; resolves; rejects with the same error object; a plain string rejection passes through; no `invalidateQueries` call; a failed delete calls the service exactly once (plain `new QueryClient()` with no mutation defaults, so it really proves retry is 0); the delete still runs while the browser is offline; an offline failure rejects with the same error (`onlineManager.setOnline(false)`, restored in `afterEach`). One test in `MassScoreEntryTool.delete.test.tsx`: after a good delete, `removeMatch` runs before the toast and the toast before `invalidateAllDataQueries` (`mock.invocationCallOrder`). The 3 existing delete tests (success, cancel, failure keeps the dialog open) were not edited and pass.
- **Gates.** `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` and `npm run knip` exit 0. `prettier --check` is clean on the touched code files. New hook test plus `src/components/admin/__tests__`: 12 files, 54 tests pass. **CI's coverage command** (`CI=true VITEST_DEEPSOURCE=1 VITEST_CI_COVERAGE=1 VITEST_FAST_COVERAGE=1 npx vitest run --coverage`): exit 0. **892 files pass. 8,558 tests pass and 15 are expected fail, 8,573 in all.** Coverage: statements 89.99, branches 81.09, functions 89.83, lines 91.53. `TESTING.md` now says 892 files / 8,573 tests (15 expected-fail) in both places. Measured on this tree: the 3H numbers (891 / 8,564) plus 1 file and 9 tests.
- **Recount (measured with grep).** **42** non-test files in `src/components` and `src/pages` still import `@/services` (43 after 3H). **The 13-file "own mutation" count is now 13 of 13 done.** The lint rule "components must not import services" STILL cannot go on: the 42 remaining files (type-only imports, pure helpers, colocated hooks, the bracket-admin helpers) are not handled yet. Slice 9 decides: an exception list or fixes.
- **Not checked (no browser here).** Nobody has watched a delete in a browser since the move: Mass Score Entry as admin, delete a match, confirm, see "Match deleted", the row gone, the dialog closed. The unit tests cover the wiring, not the look.
- **Rollback.** Revert the code commit. No stored data changes. A match already deleted stays deleted, as before.
- **Open follow-ups (not built, not part of this slice).**
  1. **Deleting a match leaves NO audit record.** VERIFIED: `matches` has no audit trigger, and the live-scoring audit skips matches. (Same before and after 3J.)
  2. **UNKNOWN: a match delete may remove stored `team_season_stats` rows of ARCHIVED seasons.** The reviewers found two AFTER DELETE triggers on `matches` in the repo: `trigger_cleanup_orphaned_stats` (statement level, migration 20260108005302) and `cleanup_team_stats_on_match_delete` (row level, migration 20260112161524). The statement-level one looks only at `matches`, not `matches_archive`, so it may drop stats rows of archived seasons on any match delete. UNKNOWN whether these triggers are live. Needs a read-only live check by `supabase-data`. Same before and after 3J.
  3. **A deleted playoff match leaves the bracket stale.** INFERRED (existing behavior, not re-run).
  4. **`match_comments` and `match_reactions` have no ON DELETE cascade, so deleting a match that has comments may fail with 23503.** INFERRED from the baseline migration, not re-checked against later migrations.

## Slice 3I result (built 2026-11-20 on `claude/adoring-bohr-waxxok`; 1 code commit + 1 docs commit; no migration, no SQL, nothing to hand-apply)

**Answer first.** The Create Bracket pop-up no longer builds its own `useMutation`. That code moved into a new hook, `useCreateBracket`. **Nothing changes for players or admins.** The same service function runs, the same three lists refresh, the same two log lines are written. No scoring, seeding or bracket logic was touched.

- **What changed.**
  - New `src/hooks/playoffs/useCreateBracket.ts`. It holds `useMutation` + `useQueryClient`. `mutationFn` is `createBracket` itself (a bare reference, not an arrow wrapper). `onSuccess` is the dialog's old block copied as it was: `bracketLog('Bracket created successfully:', bracket)`, then `invalidateQueries` for `['brackets']`, `['playoff-data']` and `['playoff-matches']`, in that order, **not awaited and not returned**. `onError` is `errorLog('Failed to create bracket:', error)`. The hook shows no toast and returns the mutation object, like `useUpdateBracket`.
  - `src/components/playoffs/BracketCreationDialog.tsx` now says `const createBracketMutation = useCreateBracket();`. Two imports went away (`useMutation` and `createBracket` from `@/services/bracket-creator`). Nothing else in the file changed.
- **Kept on purpose: the duplicate refresh.** The hook refreshes the three lists in `onSuccess`. The dialog still does its own `invalidateQueries` + `refetchQueries` (in a `Promise.all`) after `mutateAsync`. That looks like a double refresh. It is not changed because the dialog's copy is awaited (it also refetches, and the jump to the new bracket waits on it), and the hook's copy is not awaited (the mutation must not wait on the refresh, so the success toast is not delayed). Merging them would change that timing. A plain move keeps both.
- **Keys unchanged.** `['brackets']`, `['playoff-data']`, `['playoff-matches']`: the same literals as before. The repo has no query-key constants file, so none was added.
- **NOT off the future lint rule list.** The dialog still imports two things from `@/services`: the type `BracketFormData` and the pure function `BracketValidationService.validateFormData`. They were left in place on purpose. They go on the exception list of the future components-to-hooks lint rule, like `useToast`. Recount (measured): **44** non-test files in `src/components` and `src/pages` still import `@/services`, the same as after 3E + 3F. So the lint rule still cannot go on.
- **13-file "own mutation" count.** At the "Slice 3D + 3G result" snapshot 6 were done and 7 remained. 3E + 3F finished 4 of them and 3I finished 1, so **2 remained then: `SeedingUpdateDialog` (3H) and `MassScoreEntryTool` (3J)**. 3H is done since: see "Slice 3H result". 3J is DONE too: see "Slice 3J result". (The 3I brief said "7 left to 6 left", which counts from the 3E + 3F step. Counting from the 13, 11 are done.)
- **Tests added: 1 file, 4 tests** (`src/hooks/playoffs/__tests__/useCreateBracket.test.ts`, real `QueryClient`, service and logger mocked).
  - `mutateAsync(payload)` calls `createBracket` once with that exact payload as the first argument and resolves with the new bracket.
  - On success `invalidateQueries` is called with each of the three keys (3 calls in all, each key asserted on its own), and `bracketLog` gets the success message.
  - On failure the call rejects with the same error, `errorLog('Failed to create bracket:', error)` is called, and `invalidateQueries` and `bracketLog` are not.
  - The mutation settles without waiting for the refresh. The test makes each `invalidateQueries` call return a promise that stays pending, then checks that `mutateAsync` still resolves. It releases the pending promises in a `finally`. (Added after the review nitpick on PR #1775: the other 3 tests use a mock that resolves at once, so they could not see an added `await`.)
  - Existing tests not edited: `BracketCreationDialog.navigation.test.tsx` and `playoffBracketCreationAndAdvancement.integration.test.tsx` mock `@/services/bracket-creator` by module path, so they pass as they were.
- **Mutation checks (each run on the real hook file, then restored byte for byte).**
  - Key `'brackets'` changed to `'bracket'`: the refresh test fails (the other 2 stay green).
  - `errorLog` call removed: the failure test fails (the other 2 stay green).
  - `mutationFn` changed to an arrow wrapper that copies the payload (`createBracket({ ...input })`): the "exact payload" test fails (`toBe` is an identity check).
  - **Caught by the 4th test:** `onSuccess` made `async` with `await Promise.all([...])` around the three `invalidateQueries` calls. "settles without waiting for the cache refresh" fails (the other 3 stay green). This is the timing rule above. Without that test, all 3 first tests stay green, because their mock resolves at once. The code comment in the hook still explains the rule.
- **Gates.** `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` and `npm run knip` exit 0. `prettier --check` is clean on the touched files. The new test, `BracketCreationDialog.navigation.test.tsx` and the integration test pass. `src/components/playoffs` and `src/hooks/playoffs`: 83 files, 705 tests pass. **CI's coverage command** (`CI=true VITEST_DEEPSOURCE=1 VITEST_CI_COVERAGE=1 VITEST_FAST_COVERAGE=1 npx vitest run --coverage`): exit 0. **890 files pass. 8,539 tests pass and 15 are expected fail, 8,554 in all** (re-measured after the 4th test was added; the first measurement with 3 tests was 8,538 passed, 8,553 in all). Coverage: statements 89.99, branches 81.09, functions 89.82, lines 91.52. `TESTING.md` now says 890 files / 8,554 tests (15 expected-fail) in both places. These counts were measured on this tree, not added to a remembered number. Note: they differ from the earlier numbers in this log (898 files / 8,692 tests in the "Slice 3D + 3G result" section) and from the old `TESTING.md` line (889 / 8,550). The cause was not investigated (UNKNOWN). The old `TESTING.md` line plus this slice's 1 file and 4 tests equals the measured count exactly.
- **Not checked (no browser here).** Nobody has watched a bracket being created since the move: Create Bracket as admin at 360 px, "Bracket Created Successfully", the jump to the new bracket one second later. The unit tests cover the wiring, not the look.
- **Rollback.** Revert the code commit. No stored data changes, and the query-key strings are the same, so nothing is left stale in the cache.

## Slice 3H result (built 2026-11-20 on `claude/adoring-bohr-waxxok`; 1 code commit + 1 docs commit; no migration, no SQL, nothing to hand-apply)

**Answer first.** The Update Seeding pop-up no longer calls the bracket service itself. The save call moved into a new hook, `useUpdateSeeding`. **Nothing changes for admins.** The same service function runs with the same inputs. The `canUpdate` lock, the drag logic and the toasts are untouched. No scoring, seeding or bracket logic was touched.

- **What moved.**
  - New `src/hooks/playoffs/useUpdateSeeding.ts`: a `useMutation` whose `mutationFn` is `(options) => bracketManagerService.updateSeeding(options)`. No `onSuccess`, no `onError`, no toast, no refresh, no `errorLog`.
  - `src/components/playoffs/SeedingUpdateDialog.tsx`: `const updateSeeding = useUpdateSeeding();` and `await updateSeeding.mutateAsync({ bracketId, newSeeding: teams, keepSameSize: true })`. The `@/services` import is gone, so the dialog has zero `@/services` imports. Everything else is the same: `setIsSubmitting`, the success toast, the three not-awaited `invalidateQueries` (`['bracket', bracketId]`, `['brackets']`, `['bracket-participants', bracketId]`) in that order after the toast, `onOpenChange(false)`, the catch toast, the `finally`. `isSubmitting` was not switched to `isPending`.
- **Design choice 1: the refresh stays in the dialog.** Unlike 3I, the hook does not refresh anything. The admin must see the toast first, then the lists refresh, then the pop-up closes. Putting the refresh in the hook's `onSuccess` would run it before the toast. A test now pins the order.
- **Design choice 2: an arrow wrapper, not a bare reference.** `updateSeeding` is a class method that reads `this.seedingService` (`BracketManagerService.ts:208`). A bare `mutationFn: bracketManagerService.updateSeeding` loses `this` and fails at run time.
- **Design choice 3: `networkMode: 'always'`.** TanStack v5 pauses a mutation while the browser is offline. Before this slice the dialog called the service directly, so an offline save failed at once and showed the "Update failed" toast. Without this line the dialog would hang on "saving" and could run later with a stale seeding order. Found in the CodeRabbit review of PR #1776 (VERIFIED: no `networkMode` default anywhere in `src`, and the app `QueryClient` in `App.tsx` sets no mutation defaults).
- **One new side effect (harmless).** The save now runs through `useMutation`, so the app-wide `MutationCache` `onError` in `App.tsx` also sees a failed re-seed. It adds one `mutation_error` metric and one error-log line. It shows no extra toast. The hook adds no log of its own, so there is no double log.
- **Mutation checks (each run on the real file, then restored byte for byte; `git diff` confirmed).**
  - (i) `mutationFn` changed to the bare reference: all 7 hook tests fail and 3 dialog save tests fail ("this was lost"). The `this` test is among them.
  - (ii) `keepSameSize: true` changed to `false` in the dialog: 2 existing dialog tests fail ("sends the current order and closes on success", "leaves legacy BYE rows out of the seeding it submits").
  - (iii) an `invalidateQueries` added to the hook (`onSuccess`): "does not refresh any query itself" fails, the other 6 hook tests stay green. (A first try had a syntax slip that failed all 7; it was redone properly.)
  - (iv) the three invalidations moved above the toast in the dialog: the new order test fails, the other 12 dialog tests stay green.
  - (v) the `networkMode: 'always'` line removed from the hook: both offline tests fail (the paused save never reaches the service), the other 7 hook tests stay green.
- **Tests added: 10.** `src/hooks/playoffs/__tests__/useUpdateSeeding.test.ts` (9 tests, real `QueryClient` with `mutations: { retry: false }`, service mocked with a method that reads `this`): exact options object passed once (`toBe`), `this` kept, resolves to undefined, rejects with the same error object, a plain string rejection passes through, no `invalidateQueries` call, a failed save is called exactly once, the save still runs while the browser is offline, an offline failure rejects with the same error (`onlineManager.setOnline(false)`, restored in `afterEach`). One test in `SeedingUpdateDialog.test.tsx`: after a good save the success toast comes before the three `invalidateQueries` calls (call order), and the three keys are right. Existing dialog tests were not edited and pass.
- **Gates.** `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` and `npm run knip` exit 0. `prettier --check` clean on the touched files. (Lint first asked for an import sort in 2 files; fixed with `--fix`.) ## Slice 3 PR-3 result (team edit save checks the row), built 2026-11-18 on `claude/adoring-bohr-waxxok`; 1 code commit + 1 docs commit

Doug asked for this fix. It is a deliberate behavior change.

- **What changed.** One function: `updateTeamNameAndImage` in `src/services/teams/TeamUpdateService.ts`. It now asks the database for the updated row back (`.select('id').maybeSingle()`). If no row comes back, it throws `BusinessLogicError`. The database-error check still runs first.
- **Why.** Row-level security can hide the team row from a member whose approval was removed in another tab. In that case the database returns no error and 0 rows. The old code only looked at the error, so the screen said "Team Updated" and nothing was saved. (VERIFIED by reading the code and the tests. The database behaviour is INFERRED from how PostgREST works; nothing was run against a real database.)
- **What the member sees now.** The existing red toast headed "Couldn't update team" reads: "Failed to update team details: Nothing was saved. You may no longer be able to edit this team. Refresh the page and try again." The editor stays open. `refreshMembership` is not called. The existing `TeamEditSection` test already pins that for any thrown error.
- **Tests.** In `src/services/teams/__tests__/TeamUpdateService.test.ts` the 3 `updateTeamNameAndImage` tests now use a mock chain `update -> eq -> select -> maybeSingle`. Every old assertion stays. New: the happy path also asserts `eq('id', teamId)` and `select('id')`. New test: `{ data: null, error: null }` rejects with `BusinessLogicError` matching /Nothing was saved/. The database-error test returns `{ data: null, error }`, so it also proves the error check runs before the null check.
- **Mutation checks (red, then green after restore).**
  - Remove the `if (!updated)` throw: the new 0-row test goes red ("promise resolved instead of rejecting").
  - Move the null check above the `if (error)` line: the database-error test goes red (it gets `BusinessLogicError`, not `DatabaseError`).
  - Drop `.select('id')`: 4 tests go red, because the mock chain has no `maybeSingle` on the result of `eq`. The `select('id')` assertion is covered by the same change, but the first failure is a broken chain, not that assertion.
- **Counts.** One test added. `TESTING.md`: 898 files / 8,693 tests (was 8,692).
- **Docs.** `docs/product-description/teams/my-team.md` said the failure toast was headed "Update Failed". The real heading is "Couldn't update team". Fixed, plus one sentence for the new case, and the reason wording now says a plain database failure reads "Failed to update team details. Please try again." (checked against `getUIErrorMessage`). Row MYTEAM-27 in `verification/home-and-teams.md` got the same heading fix. Its status is unchanged and nothing was marked verified. The other "Update Failed" mentions in the product description belong to other screens and were left alone.
- **NOT changed.**
  - `TeamEditSection.tsx`, `useUpdateTeamDetails.ts` and the admin `updateTeamApi`.
  - No migration, so nothing to hand-apply. No history impact.
  - No SQL RLS test (optional; it needs `SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated`) and no new component test for the message text.
  - No automatic refresh after the failure (recommendation: not for now).
- **Same hole elsewhere (NOT fixed; list only).** Each cite was read in the current files. A write whose row is hidden by row-level security, or whose id no longer matches, returns no error and does nothing.
  - Member paths:
    - `src/services/messages/MessageService.ts:85-93` (`updateMessage`, edit your own message).
    - `src/services/teams/TeamMembershipService.ts:79-92` (a member re-requests or switches team).
    - `src/services/liveScoring/LiveMatchService.ts:149-175` (`completeGame`, `reopenGame`).
  - Admin-only paths:
    - `src/services/contact/ContactRequestService.ts:42-58` (`markResolved`, `reopen`).
    - `src/services/support/SupportTicketService.ts:99-107` (`markResolved`, `reopen`).
    - `src/services/BlindDrawService.ts:38-42` (confirmation message update).
  - **DONE in Holes PR A (see "Holes PR A result" below):** everything on this list except the live-scoring pair (PR B).
- **Slice 3 status.** Slice 3 was then done except 3H-3J (`SeedingUpdateDialog`, `BracketCreationDialog`, `MassScoreEntryTool`). Those needed Doug's OK first. 3I (`BracketCreationDialog`) and 3H (`SeedingUpdateDialog`) are done since: see "Slice 3I result" and "Slice 3H result". **3J is DONE too, see "Slice 3J result".**

Component: 2 new cases that pick Hawks in the real dropdown (failure keeps Hawks and the button stays on; success shows "Select a team to join" and the button goes off). **Mutation checks (red, then green):** flipping the condition in the screen turns both component cases red; making the failure path return `true` turns the hook failure case red. Both files were restored byte for byte (`git diff` shows only the intended change).
- **Docs.** `join-a-team.md` (Submit section) no longer says the dropdown is cleared after a failure. Verification row JOIN-21 already expected the chosen team to be kept, so it is unchanged. Nothing is marked verified by hand.

## Holes PR C3 result (bracket edit, bracket delete and legacy playoff score saves check the row), built 2026-11-20 on `claude/adoring-bohr-waxxok`; 1 commit; no migration, nothing to hand-apply

Answer first: **editing a bracket, deleting a bracket and saving a score on a legacy playoff match now show a red error when the database blocks the write. Before, they showed success and nothing changed.** Same pattern as Holes PR A, B, C1 and C2: the database-error check runs first, then "no row came back" throws `BusinessLogicError`. `.eq('id', ...)` is the only filter. **No status filter was added.** No stored value changes. No write order changes. No scoring and no bracket structure change.

- **What changed (VERIFIED by reading the code and running the tests; the database behaviour is INFERRED from how PostgREST works, nothing was run against a real database).** `src/services/brackets/BracketWriteService.ts`: `updateBracket` and `updatePlayoffMatchScores` now end in `.select('id').maybeSingle()`, `deleteBracket` is `.delete().eq('id', ...).select('id').maybeSingle()`. Each throws when the row is null.
- **Messages.**
  - `updateBracket`: "Nothing was saved. This bracket may have been deleted, or your admin access may have changed. Refresh the page and try again."
  - `deleteBracket`: "Nothing was deleted. This bracket may already be gone, or your admin access may have changed. Refresh the page and try again."
  - `updatePlayoffMatchScores`: "Nothing was saved. This match may have been deleted, or your admin access may have changed. Refresh the page and try again."
- **Callers (VERIFIED).**
  - `updateBracket` is called only by `useUpdateBracket.ts`. Its `onError` toasts "Could not update bracket" with `error.message`, so the message shows as written. `EditBracketDialog` closes only in `onSuccess`, so on a blocked save the dialog stays open.
  - `deleteBracket` is called only by `usePlayoffPageData.deleteBracket`. **It wraps every error in a plain `Error`**, and `getUIErrorMessage` treats a plain `Error` as generic. So `usePlayoffViewState.handleConfirmDeleteBracket` would have shown only "Failed to delete bracket. Please try again." (this already hid every specific reason, not only the new one). Fix: `usePlayoffViewState.ts` now reads the reason from `error.cause` when there is one. The toast now reads "Delete failed" with "Failed to delete bracket: Nothing was deleted. ...". The delete dialog stays open after a throw (it did before; the existing test pins it). A **double click** on Delete: the second call finds nothing, so it now says "already gone" instead of silently succeeding. That is acceptable: the first call already deleted the bracket, and the dialog and list refresh on that first success.
  - `updatePlayoffMatchScores` is called only by the legacy branch of `usePlayoffMatchUpdate.ts` (brackets not built with the current engine; the playoffs page does not open those today, see `the-playoffs-page.md` open questions). **Exact order in that branch (VERIFIED, unchanged by this commit):** 1. `fetchPlayoffMatchTeams`; 2. `updatePlayoffMatchScores` (the changed call); 3. `replacePlayoffGames` (RPC, only when games exist); 4. `invalidateMatchRelatedQueries`; 5. success toast "Score saved". Nothing advances winners in this branch (no auto-progression). A throw at step 2 stops steps 3 to 5, so a refused score no longer writes games and no longer shows "Score saved". The caller `usePlayoffEditMatch.handleSaveMatchScore` already rolls back the optimistic score and `useOptimisticScoreMutation.onError` toasts "Update Failed" with "Score update failed: Nothing was saved. ...". The current-engine branch uses `bracketManagerService.updateMatch` and is untouched.

## Holes PR C4 result (team delete removes the team row before its images), built 2026-11-20 on `claude/adoring-bohr-waxxok`; 1 commit; no migration, nothing to hand-apply

Answer first: **deleting a team now removes the team row first and its logo and image files second. Before, the files went first, so a delete the database refused left the team with no images.** If the row delete fails, storage is never touched. **A second click on Delete (0 rows) does not throw and does not touch storage.** No status filter. No stored value changes. No scoring.

- **What changed (VERIFIED by reading the code and running the tests; the database behaviour is INFERRED from how PostgREST works, nothing was run against a real database).** `deleteTeamApi` (`src/services/teams/TeamDeleteService.ts`).
  - **Order before:** 1. list files under `teams/<id>`; 2. remove them; 3. delete the row. **Order after:** 1. `delete().eq('id', ...).select('id')` (array form); 2. only if at least 1 row came back, list and remove the files (best effort).
  - A database error goes through `handleDatabaseError` ("Failed to delete team from database") and nothing else runs.
  - The cleanup is unchanged: same list and remove calls, same `teamLog` messages, `warnLog` on a list or remove error, never throws. The old comment "continue with team deletion even if file cleanup fails" now reads that leftover files are only wasted space, because the team is already gone.
  - JSDoc: row first, then best-effort cleanup; `@throws DatabaseError` only when the row delete fails.
- **Why no throw on 0 rows.** A second click on Delete is legitimate (the first click already deleted the team, and the list refreshes on that success), so it must stay quiet. A 0-row result can also be a permission block. In both cases we cannot tell if the team still exists, so storage is not touched. A comment in the code says this.
- **Callers (VERIFIED).** The only caller is `useTeamMutations.ts` (`mutationFn: deleteTeamApi`). `onSuccess` refreshes the team queries; `onError` only logs. `useTeamManagement.handleDeleteTeam` awaits it, then shows "Team Deleted" or, in `catch`, "Deletion Failed" with `getUIErrorMessage(error, ...)`. The confirm button is a Radix `AlertDialogAction`, which probably closes the dialog either way (INFERRED, not run in a browser; this was already so before C4), so the admin may need to reopen it to retry. **Nothing depends on the images going before the row, and nothing depends on an error from the cleanup (it never threw).** The error toast still shows (existing `useTeamManagement` and `useTeamMutations` tests pass, unchanged).
- **Orphan files (VERIFIED by search).** No database trigger or other service removes team files: the only `storage.from('teams').remove` call is this one, and the migrations only hold storage access policies (delete on the `teams` bucket is allowed to admins and approved members of that team's folder, `20260515152444_...sql`). So if the cleanup fails after the row is gone, the files stay as unused orphans. That is accepted: best effort, warn only. They cost space only. The `teams/<id>` folder name is the deleted team's id, so nothing reuses it. No sweep exists, and none was added.
- **Tests.** `TeamDeleteService.test.ts` rewritten for the chain `delete -> eq -> select` (6 tests, was 4): success (row delete first, then list, then remove, checked with `invocationCallOrder`; `eq('id', ...)` and `select('id')` checked); **KEY: database error gives `DatabaseError` and `storage.from`, list and remove are never called**; 0 rows resolves and storage is never called; list error after the row delete still resolves; remove error after the row delete still resolves; no files resolves and remove is not called. **Mutation check (red, then green):** with the old service file, 3 of 6 go red (order, database error, 0 rows); the file was restored byte for byte.
- **What this means on league night.** An admin who deletes a team and is refused (the team still has matches, or access changed) now keeps the team's logo. Before, the team stayed with a broken logo. A double click on Delete stays quiet.
- **Docs.** One line in `docs/product-description/admin/manage-teams-and-divisions.md` (the team delete note). The `TeamDeleteService` line of the Holes PR A report-only list is marked DONE. Nothing was marked verified. No verification row described the delete order.
- **NOT changed.** Hooks, UI, the toasts, `ChallongeFallbackService`, `types.ts`, `supabase/`, scoring, tie-breaks. No history impact. The other report-only holes in the Holes PR A list are still open.
- **Counts.** 2 tests added, no files added. CI coverage run (`CI=true VITEST_DEEPSOURCE=1 VITEST_CI_COVERAGE=1 VITEST_FAST_COVERAGE=1 npx vitest run --coverage`): exit 0, 891 files, 8,529 passed plus 15 expected-fail (8,544 total; was 8,542). `TESTING.md` now says 891 files / 8,544 tests in both places. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` and `npm run knip` were clean.
- **Rollback.** Revert the commit. Nothing is stored.

## Holes PR C5 result (delete round checks the row), built 2026-11-20 on `claude/adoring-bohr-waxxok`; 1 commit; no migration, nothing to hand-apply

Answer first: **deleting a round in Live Corrections now shows a red error when nothing was deleted. Before, it showed "Round deleted" and nothing changed.** A round is scored data (it feeds the game totals), so a false "deleted" is misleading. Doug approved an error. Same pattern as Holes PR A, B and C1 to C4: the database-error check runs first, then "no row came back" throws `BusinessLogicError`. `.eq('id', ...)` is the only filter. **No status filter was added.** No stored value changes. No scoring.

- **What changed (VERIFIED by reading the code and running the tests; the database behaviour is INFERRED from how PostgREST works, nothing was run against a real database).** `AdminCorrectionsService.deleteRound` (`src/services/liveScoring/AdminCorrectionsService.ts`) is now `.delete().eq('id', roundId).select('id').maybeSingle()`. `if (error) handleLiveScoringError(...)` stays first. A short JSDoc with `@throws` was added. `updateRound` and `setGameWinner` were not touched.
- **Message.** "Nothing was deleted. This round may already be gone, or your admin access may have changed. Refresh the page and try again."
- **Which situations reach the throw (VERIFIED by reading `seasonIdForRound`).** The function first runs `assertSeasonEditable(await seasonIdForRound(roundId))`. `seasonIdForRound` uses `maybeSingle()` and returns `undefined` when the round is already gone. It does **not** throw NotFound. `assertSeasonEditable` returns early for an empty season id. So the delete is still attempted, and 0 rows now reach the new throw in all three cases:
  - **The round was already gone before the admin pressed Delete** (deleted in another tab or by another admin; the panel still showed it). This is the common case, not only a race.
  - **A race between the two calls** (the round vanishes between the season read and the delete).
  - **The database blocks the delete** (row-level security, admin access removed). The season read can still succeed in that case, because reading rounds is probably allowed (INFERRED; the policies were not run).
  - Not reached: an archived season (the earlier `BusinessLogicError` throws first), and a database error (goes through `handleLiveScoringError`).
- **Callers (VERIFIED).** The only caller is `useAdminCorrections.ts` `deleteRound` (used by `MatchCorrectionDialogs.tsx:86`).
  - The hook's `onError` already toasts "Could not delete round" with `description: getUIErrorMessage(error)`. `getUIErrorMessage` shows a `BusinessLogicError` message as written (`errorHandler.ts` `describeError`, default branch). So the new text reaches the screen. **No hook change, no hook test.**
  - `onSuccess` ("Round deleted") does not run on a throw. `onSettled` (the re-read of the match and the drift card) still runs, so the list refreshes and an already-gone round disappears. That is what the message tells the admin to do.
  - The dialog: `onConfirm` awaits `mutateAsync`, so `clearSelection` after it does not run on a throw. But `DeleteRoundDialog` uses a Radix `AlertDialogAction`, which probably closes the dialog on click either way (INFERRED, not run in a browser). The same was true for every earlier failure of this dialog.
  - **Odd, report only (not changed):** `onConfirm` is an async function passed to a `() => void` prop, so the rejected `mutateAsync` becomes an unhandled promise rejection in the console. This is not new (every failed delete did this). The toast still shows.
- **`MatchResultDriftService` (VERIFIED).** The comment at ~:213 only says `deleteRound` does not re-decide the game. Nothing there calls `deleteRound`, and nothing depends on a silent 0-row delete. The drift card still refreshes through the hook's `onSettled`.
- **Tests.** `AdminCorrectionsService.test.ts`: the delete mock chain is now `delete -> eq -> select -> maybeSingle` (the `wire` helper takes `deleted` and `deleteError`).
  - **Flipped:** "allows the write when the round is already gone, so the delete no-ops" is now "reports an error when the round is already gone and the delete removes nothing" (expects `BusinessLogicError` matching `/Nothing was deleted/`).
  - Success test also asserts `eq('id', 'r1')` and `select('id')`. The archived-season and no-season tests are unchanged.
  - New: 0 rows with the round still found (database blocks the delete) throws; a database error throws `DatabaseError`. Net: 2 tests added (46 to 48 in the file).
  - **Mutation check (red, then green):** turning the throw off makes the 2 zero-row tests go red; the file was restored byte for byte.
- **What this means on league night.** If two admins fix the same round, or an admin's access changed, the second delete now says nothing was deleted and to refresh. Before, it said "Round deleted". Game totals and winners are not touched by this change. A delete that works behaves as before.
- **Audit trail (VERIFIED by search).** The audit triggers (`20260701120000_admin_audit_triggers.sql`) list `seasons`, `divisions`, `teams`, `team_timeslots`, `brackets`, `admin_notifications`, `hero_cards`, `theme_settings` and `contact_requests`. **`games` and `match_rounds` are not on the list**, so a deleted or edited round leaves no record of who did it. A trigger added by hand in the dashboard would not show in the repo (UNKNOWN). This matches the existing product docs ("Nothing records that a correction happened", `LIVEFIX-24`). Not changed here; adding triggers is its own migration and needs Doug's say. **CLOSED 2026-11-20: see "Live-scoring audit trail result" below (own migration, Doug decided).**
- **Docs.** One sentence in `docs/product-description/admin/correct-a-live-match.md` (Submit). The `deleteRound` line of the Holes PR A report-only list is marked DONE, and the whole report-only follow-up (C1 to C5) is marked finished there. No verification row described this failure, so none changed. Nothing was marked verified.
- **NOT changed.** `updateRound`, `setGameWinner`, the hook, the dialogs, `types.ts`, `supabase/`, scoring, tie-breaks. No history impact. **Still open from the report-only list after C5:** `SupabaseSqlStorage` update-by-id (deferred on purpose), `createDivision` and `ChallongeFallbackService.deleteBracket` (the last two are DONE in "Holes PR C6 result").
- **Counts.** CI coverage run (`CI=true VITEST_DEEPSOURCE=1 VITEST_CI_COVERAGE=1 VITEST_FAST_COVERAGE=1 npx vitest run --coverage`): exit 0, 891 files, 8,531 passed plus 15 expected-fail (8,546 total; was 8,544). `TESTING.md` now says 891 files / 8,546 tests in both places. `coverage/deepsource/lcov.info` shows the new throw lines of `AdminCorrectionsService.ts` covered. `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` and `npm run knip` were clean.

This bracket may already be gone, or your admin access may have changed. Refresh the page and try again." Its `updateBracket` was already fine and is untouched. A JSDoc with `@throws` was added.

The shared `onError` toasts "Error" with `description: getUIErrorMessage(error, 'Failed to remove bracket')`. This pins the C2 message path that had no hook test. No hook code changed.
